          var isMsgMatch = message && list.some(function(str) {
            return message.toLowerCase().indexOf(str) > -1
          })

            return stack.toLowerCase().indexOf(str) > -1
          })

          var self = this
          var message = data && data.message || ''
          var stack = data && data.stack || ''
          return new Promise(function(resolve) {
            var isMatch = false
            if (type === 'error') {
              isMatch = self.checkInWhitelist(
                MirrorErrorFilterPlugin.errorFilterList,
                message,
                stack
              )

                message,
                stack

              }

                MirrorErrorFilterPlugin.resourceFilterList,
                message,
                stack
              )
            }

            return resolve(!isMatch)
          })
        }
